# Layout review of the main board

The review of `hardware/sunbeam-em7000/main-board` by the [Layout review](../../../docs/board-design-method.md#layout-review) step of the board design method. Each round is recorded at its end, before any change to the board it calls for. Round 1 was judged by area group, by hand, with seven reviewer files merged into one round, because the review kit's shared context for this board is about 1.4 million tokens.

## Round 1

- **Board judged**: 502f317e63cfcb7da82631eddb67e01ce05c8365
- **Views**: the commands the views were rendered with, as run:

```
task hw:views -- --board hardware/sunbeam-em7000/main-board/main-board.kicad_pcb --project hardware/sunbeam-em7000/main-board/main-board.kicad_pro --class Mains --class MainsTrunk --class MainsBranch --class Mains_Entry --class Ground --class Supply5V --class Supply3V3 --class Sense --class Fast --out /Users/david/real5/viewsN/views
task hw:placement -- --board hardware/sunbeam-em7000/main-board/main-board.kicad_pcb --project hardware/sunbeam-em7000/main-board/main-board.kicad_pro --out /Users/david/real5/viewsN/placement
```

- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` and `VIOLATION` line under it:

### Plan and free pass

- Compared the Floorplan: the rule areas in the board file (layout-facts ZONES) and the `design-rules.md` region rows equal the plan's bounds and parts lists for all four regions (mains 6 rectangles, low voltage 9, analog sensing 10, digital 15). Departures from the plan: C406 and C414 outside `digital` (findings 1, 2).
- Compared the connector table against all 43 pin 1 positions: 42 within 0.35 mm of their row, JP101 10.4 mm off (finding 3, which BOARD-16 describes). The plan's J862 row now carries pin 1 at (122, 91.5) and the board matches it.
- Compared plan row TEST-1 and TEST-2 with the board: the row's own amendments (TP501/TP502 1.56 mm, TP824/TP832 2.01 mm apart; TP529 and TP833 on F.Cu) are exactly what I measured (findings 5, 6).
- Plan row DECOUPLE-5 (each snubber pair within 3 mm of the tab land, tracks no longer than 8 mm): C701 stands 3.45 mm and R706 0.56 mm from T701's courtyard, C711 0.65 mm and R716 0.56 mm from T711's; `PUMP_C_SNUB` measures 21.1 mm and `PUMP_S_SNUB` 30.6 mm of track. Row not met (finding 7).
- Plan row BOARD-2 (C304 and C305 10 mm or more from U302's tab): C305 (84.1, 35.1) is 6.9 mm and C304 (86.35, 37.3) 8.5 mm from the tab corner (77.85, 38.1), as the plan row itself admits (finding 8). The U302 tab land and B.Cu pour are another group's measurement.
- Plan rows I checked and found met: BOARD-7 (holes), BOARD-12 and BOARD-13 as above; BOARD-11 distances from the courtyard boxes: U401 21.3 mm from the module (needs 15), the converter cells U620, U621, U630, U631 at least 20.3 mm from K201 and 23.2 mm from U301 (the U601 parts such as C612 and R609 stand 5.3 to 6.8 mm from the module, which the row allows for the 125 degree C part); BOARD-16 JP101 at (38.5, 93.9) between F101 and K201 as stated (0.55 and 0.52 mm courtyard gaps).
- Free pass: nothing further seen on the tiles opened. Observations carried by other items: D526's designator text stands inside H2's courtyard (a `designator_astray` VIOLATION, MFG-9); D521, D522 and D526 stand about 1.2 mm from the bottom board edge (courtyard box), carried in finding 4.
- Not seen: tab receptacle and relay heights are not drawn (index.txt: no model), so BOARD-13 rests on the record's heights; no net view or layer view was opened.
- Coverage by measurement only: the placement of all 456 parts against the four regions (the check's `items_not_allowed` and measures.txt OUTSIDE lines), all 84 test point spacings and sides, all 43 connector positions, the four holes' copper clearances, all courtyard gaps around the hand-fitted parts. The 45 placement tiles were not opened (only r3c4); 7 images were opened in all.
- Departure to note: the views and placement tiles were rendered from commit N d685d6b5, not the head e16d375b; the head differs from N only by one removed dangling via, three do-not-populate footprint attributes and NT601's attributes. I measured from the checkout of e16d375b (layout-facts.txt and the board file), so no positions in this file depend on the N render, and none of those changes moves a part or a test point.
- (A-mains-safety) Compared the plan rows MAINS-1 to MAINS-5, CONN-1, BOARD-3, -4, -5, -6, -9, -14, -15, -16 with the board; the plan's own notes (MAINS-2 amendment naming AMC_DIN, POL_OUT, AMC_CS_PIN, BOARD-9 open note on C101 and RV101, CONN-1 deviation note) agree with what I measured; none of them closes a finding for me.
- (A-mains-safety) Checked by measurement only (no tile opened): B.Cu and In3.Cu/In4.Cu copper of the mains block against the strips and mains rectangles, the In2.Cu and In4.Cu pour edges, the B.Cu AMC_CS_PIN track, the 25 non-measurement-block clearance lines, and the 15 clamp-to-connector routes other than D503, D505, D570, D571.
- (A-mains-safety) The views were rendered from d685d6b5; the judged head e16d375b differs by one removed dangling via, three do-not-populate footprint attributes and NT601's attributes. None touches any item here; the report in check-report.txt is the one from the early pre-order run (e16d375b), which lists no mains_layer_separation line (the NT601 lines the plan mentions are gone).

- (A-mains-safety) Free pass: a NEUTRAL taps pour on B.Cu at (26.5, 57.2) is reported by the check as isolated copper (a floating mains island); measured only, not on an opened tile, so no finding raised.

- (C-ground-emi) Free pass: connector pins whose rings merge into one opening in In1 or In4 (J625 pins 3-4, J523 pins 2-3, J821 pins 2-3, J861 pins 2-3, J630 and J521 pins in In4, J402 in In4, strips up to 9.6 mm); no Fast or Sense track crosses them, so no separate finding.

Notes above that call e16d375b the head mean this commit.
- (Owner, record) This round was judged by seven reviewer files, one per area group, merged by hand, because the review kit's shared context for this board is about 1.4 million tokens and the kit cannot run it. Each reviewer cites only the tiles it opened; a tile cited as opened was opened, and a tile not named was not. Crops for T701 and net views for BOARD-10 were not rendered, so those items rest on measurement alone.
- (Owner, record) Correction to the mains-safety note above: the 9.96 mm creepage from C601 pad 2 to K201 pin A2 (findings 1.42 and 1.75) was measured afterwards as 10.414 mm round the slot arm by three independent routes, so the pre-order check not reporting it is correct, not a gap. The verdicts and findings stand as the reviewer recorded them.

### Findings
